Real-robot deployment on a Unitree Go2 demonstrates zero-shot sim-to-real transfer: the controller navigates cluttered indoor and dynamic outdoor environments without fine-tuning, with evasive behavior consistent with the collision reduction observed in simulation.

</details>


### [402] [Stability and Comfort in Mobile Robot-Pedestrian Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17604)
*Alireza Jafari,Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向非完整移动机器人（NMR）的社会感知导航框架，结合社交力模型（SFM）和改进的投影时间-碰撞社交力模型（TSFM），在保证系统稳定性的同时提升行人在机器人穿越时的舒适感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航算法通常将行人视为动态障碍物，缺乏对主观舒适性和安全感的建模，且多数研究集中于全向移动机器人，难以适用于实际中的非完整约束机器人。此外，关于行人主观安全感受的实证研究较少。因此，需要开发能显式建模人类感知因素并适用于NMR的导航方法。

Method: 基于社交力模型（SFM）和提出的投影时间-碰撞社交力模型（TSFM），构建适用于非完整移动机器人（NMR）的导航框架；形式化描述NMR与行人及障碍物之间的交互，并在假设行人具有有界非被动性的前提下证明系统的稳定性；通过仿真优化包含舒适性与速度的混合代价函数以标定模型参数；开展人机交互实验，比较SFM、TSFM与两种遥控基线方法的表现，并收集行人主观舒适度评分，使用统计工具分析调查结果。

Result: 所提模型在理论上被证明是稳定的；仿真实验成功标定了模型参数；实验结果表明，相较于基线方法，采用SFM和TSFM的NMR显著提升了行人的主观舒适感；统计分析验证了方法的有效性；与先前研究对比显示，该方法在舒适性和导航性能方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于SFM和TSFM的社会感知导航框架适用于非完整移动机器人，能够在保障系统稳定的同时有效提升行人在公共空间中与机器人共处时的舒适感和安全感，具备在实际场景中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Mobile robots in public spaces must ensure pedestrians' comfort, and yet empirical studies of walkers' subjective safety are rare. Many classical navigation algorithms do not distinguish the walkers from dynamic obstacles and do not explicitly model subjective human factors. Moreover, most studies focus on holonomic mobile robots, whereas applications demand Nonholonomic Mobile Robots (NMR). This paper develops socially aware algorithms for NMRs, proves the stability, verifies the performance experimentally, and statistically analyzes the reported comfort. We design a framework for NMRs using Social Force Model (SFM) and the projected Time-to-collision Social Force Model (TSFM). We formalize the NMR-pedestrians' and NMR-obstacles' interactions and prove the system's stability, assuming boundedly nonpassive pedestrians. Simulations calibrate the models by maximizing a hybrid cost function of comfort and speed. Pedestrian-robot interaction experiments compare SFM and TSFM to two remote-controlled baselines and collect walkers' reported comfort. Statistical tools analyze survey results collected during the experiments. Benchmarking the algorithms against previous studies highlights the proposed methods' advantage with respect to the studied metrics. Overall, the models are stable and improve pedestrian comfort when an NMR navigates through a pedestrian crowd.

</details>


### [403] [Configuration-Induced Passive Self-Rotation for Perception-Enhanced Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17646)
*Xurui Liu,Miao Wang,Tianyu He,Linrui Yang,Xiaobin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过结构设计实现被动自旋的三旋翼飞行器，以增强在狭窄复杂环境中的感知能力，同时通过分层自主框架实现敏捷稳定的飞行控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机载传感器视场受限，限制了在狭窄和杂乱环境中自主飞行的能力，需要在不增加传感器数量的情况下提升感知覆盖范围。

Method: 利用后臂结构参数调节被动自旋的工作点，实现对扫视视场刷新率与飞行性能的平衡；设计分层自主框架（含规划与控制）以支持持续自旋下的敏捷稳定飞行，并采用引导点重规划提升任务级覆盖。

Result: 在高速轨迹跟踪、抗干扰测试及复杂环境自主导航等真实实验中验证了该方法的有效性，实现了感知增强的自主飞行。

Conclusion: 该结构诱导的被动自旋三旋翼方案为提升感知覆盖与飞行性能的平衡提供了有效解决方案，适用于复杂环境下的高效自主飞行。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in confined and cluttered environments is fundamentally limited by the restricted field of view (FoV) of onboard sensors. Passive self-rotation expands sensing coverage without additional sensors but introduces a tradeoff between swept-FoV refresh rate and flight performance. This letter presents a configuration-induced passively self-rotating tricopter for perception-enhanced autonomous flight. Firstly, the rear-arm configuration parameter is exploited to regulate the passive self-rotation operating point, providing an airframe-level mechanism for balancing swept-FoV refresh rate and flight performance. Secondly, a hierarchical autonomy framework integrating planning and control is developed to enable agile and robust autonomous flight under continuous passive self-rotation. For waypoint-based inspection, guide-point replanning is further used to improve task-level coverage. Extensive real-world experiments, including high-speed trajectory tracking, disturbance-rejection tests, and autonomous navigation in representative cluttered environments, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for perception-enhanced autonomous flight.

</details>


### [404] [HCPG-Flow:Hierarchical Contact-Progress Guidance for Flow-Policy Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17651)
*Guanghu Xie,Mingxu Li,Shuo Zhang,Yonglong Zhang,Yifan Yang,Yang Liu,Zongwu Xie,Baoshi Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HCPG-Flow是一种增强SAC-Flow的机器人操作方法，通过层次化、以物体为中心的接触进展引导，在rollout时选择最优动作，提升了任务成功率并减少了完成步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于critic的候选动作排序方法在数据收集时依赖价值估计，而这些估计可能因回放中候选动作表示不足而不可靠，因此需要更稳定的rollout-time选择机制。

Method: 提出HCPG-Flow，引入解析式的rollout-time选择器：在接触前使用末端执行器接近策略，接触后切换为任务进展策略；通过一阶任务相关距离缩减评分，对候选动作标准化打分，并采用温度控制的动作嵌入执行。

Result: 在十个模拟任务中，HCPG-Flow在两个基准上均优于SAC-Flow，Maniskill上成功率提升9.5个百分点；在四个真实机器人任务中，成功率达高且完成步数减少17.4%。

Conclusion: HCPG-Flow通过引入层次化接触进展引导和分析性动作选择机制，有效提升了基于流策略的机器人操作性能，兼具更高的成功率和执行效率。

Abstract: Flow policies can represent multimodal action distributions for robot manipulation, yet a robot must execute one action at each control step. When several proposals are sampled, critic-based ranking makes data collection depend on value estimates over candidate actions that may be weakly represented in replay. We introduce HCPG-Flow, an analytic rollout-time selector that augments SAC-Flow with hierarchical, object-centric contact-progress guidance while preserving its actor and critic objectives. HCPG switches from end-effector approach to task progress after contact, scores each proposal by the first-order reduction of a task-relevant distance, standardizes scores within the candidate set, and executes a temperature-controlled action embedding. Across ten simulated tasks, HCPG improves mean success over SAC-Flow on both benchmarks, including a 9.5 percentage-point gain on Maniskill. Four physical tasks further show high success with a 17.4% reduction in successful completion steps.Project page: https://hitxraz.github.io/HCPG-Flow/

</details>


### [405] [SLAM in Low-Light Environments: Project Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17699)
*Oleh Basystyi,Anna Stasyshyn,Oleksandr Kosovan,Yaroslav Prytula*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种SLAM系统在低光照环境下的性能，发现仅使用RGB相机的SLAM系统在缺乏惯性融合和全局优化时表现不佳，而Kimera-VIO因结合惯性信息和持续优化表现出最佳稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM基准多基于光照良好的场景，缺乏对低光照环境下纯RGB相机SLAM能力的系统评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在五个不同难度和光照条件的LaMARia数据集上，对ORB-SLAM3、DSO、Kimera-VIO、OpenVINS、DPVO和DPV-SLAM六种代表性SLAM系统进行评测，比较其绝对与相对位姿误差及控制点召回率。

Result: Kimera-VIO是唯一能完成所有五段序列的系统，相对位姿误差最低但绝对误差随时间增长；DPVO和DPV-SLAM不丢失跟踪但绝对误差达约100米；传统单目系统（ORB-SLAM3、DSO）和OpenVINS在低光复杂场景中大多失败或发散。

Conclusion: 纯RGB相机的SLAM系统要在低光环境下保持稳定，必须同时具备惯性融合和全局优化；进一步缩小性能差距可能需要针对低光设计的学习型前端或引入辅助传感。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is one of the fundamental problems in robotics, as it enables autonomous operations in real-world scenarios. Under low illumination, reduced contrast, sensor noise, and motion blur degrade both feature extraction and feature matching, while compensating with LiDAR, depth, or thermal sensors raises cost, power draw, and integration complexity. Existing benchmarks remain dominated by well-lit indoor or daylight sequences, leaving open how far SLAM with standard RGB cameras can be pushed in the dark. We benchmark six systems spanning the feature-based, direct, filter-based, and learning-based paradigms - ORB-SLAM3, DSO, Kimera-VIO, OpenVINS, DPVO, and DPV-SLAM - on five LaMARia sequences of varying difficulty and illumination, reporting absolute and relative pose error alongside control-point recall. Kimera-VIO is the only system to track all five sequences to completion, combining the lowest relative pose error with steadily growing absolute error due to the absence of loop closure; DPVO and DPV-SLAM never lose tracking but incur absolute errors of roughly 100 m under low light; and the classical monocular pipelines (ORB-SLAM3, DSO) together with the filter-based OpenVINS fail outright or diverge on most of the harder and low-light sequences. The results suggest that RGB-only SLAM maintains stable low-light tracking only when both inertial fusion and global optimization are present. Closing the remaining gap will likely require low-light-specific learned front-ends or a return to complementary sensing.

</details>


### [406] [Lifelong Multi-Subsystem Pickup and Delivery with Buffer-Limited Handover Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17724)
*Chuanlong Zang,Isabelle Barz,Anna Mannucci,Philipp Schillinger,Florian Lier,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多子系统多智能体取送（MS-MAPD-BHS）问题中，如何通过共享交接站协调载荷传输，提出了一种称为HARR的在线控制器，有效提升了系统的吞吐量和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在终身多智能体取送任务中，智能体被限制在不同区域，需通过有限缓冲区的共享交接站传递载荷，易导致阻塞和资源饥饿，因此需要有效的协调机制。

Method: 提出了Handover-Aware Reservation and Routing (HARR) 方法，结合子系统规划器，利用共享的码头预约日历和确定性滚动时域缓冲区占用预测来协调路径与资源使用。只有当码头时段空闲且缓冲区预测未超容时，才接受候选路径。

Result: 在模拟中，HARR 在中等负载下比固定码头消融方法实现了高达77%的吞吐量提升和92%的积压减少，同时相比基于令牌传递的耦合基线方法减少了规划时间。

Conclusion: 显式的接口协调机制能显著提高模块化多子系统运输系统的稳定性和效率。

Abstract: Coordinating payload transfers between subsystems is a critical challenge in lifelong Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD). We study systems where agents are confined to separate regions and must exchange payloads through shared handover stations. These stations, equipped with single docks and finite buffers, are inherently vulnerable to blocking and starvation. We formalize this problem as Multi-Subsystem MAPD with Buffer-limited Handover Stations (MS-MAPD-BHS). We then propose Handover-Aware Reservation and Routing (HARR), an online controller that couples per-subsystem planners. HARR uses a shared dock reservation calendar and a deterministic rolling-horizon projection of buffer occupancy to coordinate actions. A candidate route is accepted only if its dock interval is free and the resulting buffer occupancy projection remains within capacity. Under perfect execution, these checks ensure collision-free dock use and buffer-safe committed operations within the reservation horizon. In simulation, HARR achieves up to 77% higher throughput and 92% lower backlog than a fixed-dock ablation at moderate load, while also reducing planning time relative to a coupled station-aware Token Passing baseline. These results show that explicit interface coordination substantially improves stability in modular multi-subsystem transport.

</details>


### [407] [Seg2Grasp: A Robust Modular Suction Grasping in Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17757)
*Hye-Jung Yoon,Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Jun-Ki Lee,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Seg2Grasp的模块化管道，用于在动态和杂乱的箱体场景中实现鲁棒的吸盘抓取。该方法通过分割、抓取和分类三个步骤，结合Transformer模型、表面法线分析和细调的Mask-CLIP分类器，在真实机器人实验中表现出更高的成功率和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端学习方法在面对不熟悉或复杂的物体时表现不佳，尤其在非结构化环境中容易失败。因此需要一种更鲁棒、可扩展的方法来提升bin picking的性能。

Method: Seg2Grasp采用三步流程：1）使用基于Transformer的模型对RGB-D图像进行类别无关的物体分割；2）利用表面法线和掩码建议确定最优吸盘抓取点；3）采用微调后的开放词汇Mask-CLIP模型进行精确物体分类。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，Seg2Grasp在抓取成功率和对新物体的适应性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Seg2Grasp通过模块化设计实现了在复杂工业环境下的高效、鲁棒抓取，为自动化bin picking提供了一个可推广且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Current bin picking methods that rely heavily on end-to-end learning often falter when confronted with unfamiliar or complex objects in unstructured environments. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Seg2Grasp, a modular pipeline designed for robust suction grasping in dynamic and cluttered bin scenarios. Seg2Grasp is built on a three-step process: Segmentation, Grasping, and Classification. The Segmentation module employs a Transformer-based model to generate class-agnostic object masks from RGB-D images, ensuring accurate detection across various conditions. The Grasping module uses surface normals and mask proposals to determine the optimal suction points, enhancing grasp success. Finally, the Classification module leverages fine-tuned open-vocabulary Mask-CLIP for precise object identification, enabling versatile handling of diverse objects. Real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that Seg2Grasp outperforms existing methods in success rates and adaptability, establishing it as a powerful tool for automated bin picking in industrial settings.

</details>


### [408] [VLN-AVP: Zero-Shot Vision-Language Navigation with Hybrid Long-Short-Term Memory for Autonomous Valet Parking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17767)
*Yijian Li,Xiangru Mu,Changze Li,Hantian Shi,Jiyuan Cai,Jia Cai,Xiaoxue Liu,Yajing Sun,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为VLN-AVP的零样本自主代客泊车导航框架，结合鸟瞰图模型与视觉语言模型，无需预建地图并支持自然语言指令导航，在仿真和真实实验中均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主泊车方法依赖预建地图，难以扩展到新环境和开放词汇目标，限制了其可扩展性；受视觉语言导航启发，探索无需地图且能理解语义指令的泊车方案。

Method: 提出VLN-AVP框架，融合鸟瞰图模型的空间感知能力与视觉语言模型的通用智能，设计短期感知记忆（跟踪语义视觉线索）和长期拓扑记忆（稳定策略学习）的混合记忆系统，并构建VLN-AVP数据集与基准测试。

Result: 在包含10个高保真地下停车场场景、超1000个导航任务的数据集上，仿真中成功率比现有VLN方法提升超25%，比其他自动驾驶方法高15%以上；真实车辆实验也取得领先成功率。

Conclusion: VLN-AVP实现了无需预建地图、支持自然语言指令的零样本自主泊车，兼具高仿真性能与实际可行性，推动了AVP在开放环境中的应用发展。

Abstract: Existing methods in Autonomous Valet Parking (AVP) typically rely on pre-built maps, which severely restricts their scalability to unseen environments and open-vocabulary targets. Inspired by the application of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) tasks, we propose VLN-AVP, a zero-shot navigation framework for AVP tasks. By combining the precise spatial perception of a Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) model with the general intelligence of VLMs, our framework 1) eliminates the dependency on pre-built maps, 2) interprets semantic environmental contexts in parking scenarios, and 3) enables intuitive navigation following natural language instructions. Specifically, we introduce a hybrid memory system: a short-term perception memory tracks semantic visual cues to address the limitations of VLM's single-frame reasoning in existing methods, while a long-term topological memory facilitates stable policy learning from past experiences. To bridge the gap in existing benchmarks, we also present the VLN-AVP dataset and benchmark. Featuring 10 high-fidelity parking scenes and over 1,000 navigation episodes, it has the largest number of garage scenes to date and is the first VLN benchmark for underground parking. Extensive experiments demonstrate that in simulation, our method achieves an over 25% improvement in success rate compared to VLN methods and an over 15% improvement compared to other autonomous driving methods. Furthermore, it attains a leading success rate in real-world vehicle experiments, proving its practical feasibility.

</details>


### [409] [From Sign Language Generation to Humanoid Execution: Vision-Language Guided Retargeting with Collision Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17769)
*Nabeela Khan,Bowen Wu,Runwu Shi,Benjamin Yen,Takeshi Ashizawa,Carlos Toshinori Ishi,Takashi Minato,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统级框架，用于将手语生成（SLG）输出转化为可在人形机器人上可靠执行的运动，通过引入基于SMPL-X的体积碰撞缓解模块和视觉-语言引导的逆运动学重定向算法，解决了生成动作中的自碰撞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的手语生成系统虽然能输出高质量的3D身体动作，但在人形机器人执行时常因手部或躯干的自穿插导致逆运动学求解失败和运动不稳定，缺乏对物理可行性和感知反馈的关注。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：一是基于SMPL-X模型的体积碰撞缓解模块，将生成的动作投影到物理合理的构型；二是基于逆运动学框架并结合视觉语言模型（VLM）作为视觉评判器的重定向算法，检测并修正具身化过程中的失败模式。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法有效减少了自碰撞现象，并通过感知引导的精细化调整提升了动作在人形机器人上的可执行性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 碰撞处理与感知引导的优化是实现可靠人形机器人手语表达的关键环节，该框架为连接手语生成与机器人具身执行提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recent sign language generation (SLG) systems increasingly output dense 3D body representations, which better preserve full-body kinematics and geometry for downstream embodiment on humanoid robots. However, these generated motions frequently exhibit self-intersections such as hand-hand and hand-torso penetration. While such artifacts may be tolerated in offline rendering, they become critical in humanoid execution as they lead to infeasible inverse-kinematics (IK) solutions, collisions, and unstable retargeted trajectories. We present a system-level framework that bridges SLG outputs to humanoid joint-space execution via two components. First, we introduce a volumetric SMPL-X collision-mitigation module that projects generated signing motions toward physically plausible configurations while minimally deviating from the original trajectory. Second, we propose a vision-language-guided retargeting algorithm built on an IK backbone: a VLM serves as a visual critic over rendered humanoid motion, identifies embodiment-specific failure modes, and triggers targeted task-space corrections. Our results highlight collision handling and perception-guided refinement as key missing components for reliable humanoid signing.

</details>


### [410] [Polar Coordinate-based Differential Evolution for Moving Target Search Using Vision Sensor on Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17771)
*Thu Hang Khuat,Duy-Nam Bui,Thuy Ngan Duong,Manh Duong Phung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于极坐标微分进化的无人机搜索算法（PDE），用于提升搜救任务中的目标检测概率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在搜救的“黄金时间”内提高发现幸存者的机会，优化无人机搜索路径以增强检测概率和速度。

Method: 将目标动态建模为马尔可夫过程，结合图像质量和观察视野建立检测似然函数，利用贝叶斯理论推导目标位置的概率分布，并设计基于极坐标的微分进化算法（PDE）生成最优搜索路径。

Result: 仿真和真实无人机实验表明，PDE算法在多种搜索场景下均优于现有先进算法，具有更高的检测概率和更短的执行时间。

Conclusion: PDE算法能有效提升无人机在复杂环境下的搜索效率，具备良好的实际应用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: In search and rescue operations, there is a period known as the "golden time" during which the probability of finding the target alive is highest. The objective of this work is to propose a new search algorithm for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) with a focus on improving the detection probability and execution time. We approach this problem by first modeling target dynamics as a Markov process and the detection likelihood as a function of image quality and the observer's vision. We then employ Bayesian theory to derive a fitness function representing the probability distribution of the target's location over the search area. Finally, we introduce a new algorithm named polar coordinate-based differential evolution (PDE) to generate a UAV search path that maximizes this fitness function. The PDE algorithm utilizes polar coordinates to incorporate kinematic constraints and maneuver properties of the UAV, allowing for better exploration of the solution space. A series of simulations and comparative analyses have been conducted to evaluate the performance of the proposed algorithm. Experiments involving a real UAV have also been conducted. Results demonstrate that the PDE algorithm outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms in terms of detection probability and execution time across diverse search scenarios while remaining practical for real-world applications. The source code of the algorithm is available at https://github.com/thuhangkhuat/PDE_target_search.

</details>


### [411] [Reasoning as a Double-Edged Sword: Architecture and Cross-Stage Robustness in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17786)
*Tuan Duong Trinh,Naveed Akhtar,Basim Azam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探讨了推理步骤对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在面对扰动时鲁棒性的影响，发现具有潜在迭代推理结构的模型反而最不稳健，且现有推理监控方法在对抗攻击下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同推理设计是否能提升VLA模型对输入扰动的鲁棒性，并评估推理过程能否作为运行时的安全信号。

Method: 在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上，对三种代表不同推理模式的模型（无推理、文本链式思维、潜在迭代循环）在视觉、推理和动作阶段进行扰动测试，并评估其任务成功率及推理可读性。

Result: 潜在迭代模型在噪声和白盒攻击下性能急剧下降，鲁棒性最差；增加推理深度对其无显著改善；尽管理论上可监控推理输出，但在自适应攻击下一致性探测器表现退化至随机水平，与动作异常探测融合后也无法提高防御成功率。

Conclusion: 当前的推理机制设计可能降低模型鲁棒性，且基于输出的行为探测无法构成有效防御，任何可行防御方案必须首先满足在白盒视觉攻击下的性能上限条件。

Abstract: Does adding a reasoning step make a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model more robust to perturbation? Intuitively, a policy that reasons before acting should absorb a perturbed input better than one that maps observations directly to actions. We test this premise head-on across three models that span the reasoning spectrum (no reasoning, a text chain-of-thought, and a latent iterative loop), perturbing each at the vision, reasoning, and action stages on LIBERO and SimplerEnv. Two questions organize the study: does the reasoning design shift robustness, and can the reasoning be read back at runtime as a safety signal? We find that the latent-iterative model is by far the least robust: under both stochastic noise and white-box perturbation its task success collapses, while the other two hold. This fragility is structural rather than cumulative: varying the reasoning depth at inference barely moves it. Reasoning outputs can in principle be monitored, but the monitors fail under fair tests. A plan--action consistency probe that looks near-perfect under naive evaluation falls to chance under adaptive attack. Under matched-FPR calibration, fusing it with an action-anomaly probe never lifts defended success above undefended. Scoped to these output-level behavioral probes under white-box vision-stage attack, this ceiling is a precondition that any viable defense must first satisfy.

</details>


### [412] [A2RL V\textsubscript{max}: The A2RL autonomous racing dataset for long-range, high-speed perception and multi-vehicle interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17813)
*Marvin Klemp,Dominic Ebner,Cornelius Schröder,Davide Malvezzi,László Turányi,Riccardo Donati,Ilia Schminik,Xia Ning,Yanxin Zhou,Matthew Flagg,Christoph Stiller,Markus Lienkamp,Marko Bertogna,Gergely Bári,Andreas Birk,Ren Jin,Chen Lv,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: A2RL V_max是一个专为高速自动驾驶和多车交互感知任务设计的开源数据集，采集自2024年阿布扎比自动驾驶赛车联赛，包含近3万帧专业标注的LiDAR和RADAR点云数据，是首个大规模、高精度标注的自动驾驶赛车场景数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶感知数据集主要集中于城市结构化道路，缺乏针对高速、多车动态交互场景（如自动驾驶赛车）的数据支持，限制了相关算法的研究与发展。

Method: 数据集在F1赛道环境下采集，涵盖单辆车不同速度行驶、多车会车及四车全程比赛等多种场景，提供高质量标注的LiDAR与RADAR点云，并以开发者友好的格式发布，支持即插即用的3D检测与跟踪方法实现与评估。

Result: 提供了近30,000帧专业标注的LiDAR点云和同步RADAR数据，支持多种感知任务；实验验证了现成3D检测与跟踪方法的有效性，但也表明高速场景下仍需专用算法来应对独特挑战。

Conclusion: A2RL V_max填补了高速自动驾驶与多车交互感知领域的大规模标注数据空白，推动了基于深度学习的自动驾驶赛车感知研究，并为未来算法开发提供了开放、可靠且易于使用的基准平台。

Abstract: In autonomous driving development, a perception dataset is crucial, as it provides fundamental data for training, testing, and validating algorithms for an autonomous vehicle's multimodal perception systems. So far, most research has concentrated on providing datasets for well-structured urban environments. This work introduces the A2RL V\textsubscript{max} open-source dataset, specifically designed for perception tasks in high-speed autonomous driving and multi-vehicle interaction. The dataset was captured during the 2024 Abu Dhabi Autonomous Racing League (A2RL), held at the Yas Marina F1 Circuit, with participation from all competing teams. It contains diverse scenarios, including single-vehicle data at varying speeds, multi-vehicle sessions, and the full final four-vehicle race. The dataset contains almost 30,000 professionally annotated LiDAR point clouds, along with RADAR point clouds. In particular, it is the first large-scale dataset in autonomous racing to feature professionally annotated LiDAR point clouds, enabling deep learning-based perception research. The data is provided in a developer-friendly format, enabling easy implementation and evaluation in future research. We provide implementation and evaluation for off-the-shelf 3D detection and tracking methods. Although baseline methods show promising results for both 3D detection and tracking, specialized methods are required to address the unique challenges of high-speed autonomous driving. For a detailed description of the dataset, please visit the \href{https://tum-avs.github.io/A2RL_Dataset_website/}{A2RL V\textsubscript{max} Dataset Website}

</details>


### [413] [Task-Space Constrained Stochastic Trajectory Optimization for Time-Optimal Forestry Crane Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17818)
*Marc-Philip Ecker,Christoph Fröhlich,Bernhard Bischof,Wolfgang Kemmetmüller,Tobias Glück*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务空间约束的VP-STO（TSC-VP-STO）算法，用于在液压泵流量限制下实现林业起重机的高效、无碰撞且时间最优的运动规划，通过放宽末端关节配置的约束，优化轨迹和冗余自由度，实现了更短的轨迹时间和更好的泵流量利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VP-STO算法需要预先固定末端关节配置，限制了对运动冗余的利用，尤其是在受非线性、全局耦合的液压泵流量约束下，无法充分优化运动性能。

Method: 提出TSC-VP-STO算法，将末端关节空间约束替换为任务空间约束，联合优化轨迹与终端冗余自由度；通过构型空间分解和可达性约束建模，适应环境依赖的运动与液压分配。

Result: 实验表明，相比VP-STO，TSC-VP-STO平均缩短轨迹时间12-15%，并提升了泵流量利用率；实际部署验证了其在真实林业起重机上的可行性。

Conclusion: TSC-VP-STO通过任务空间约束有效利用了机械臂的冗余自由度，在复杂液压约束下实现了更高效的时间最优运动规划，适用于实际林业作业场景。

Abstract: Efficient, collision-free, and time-optimal motion planning is a fundamental requirement for autonomous forestry cranes operating under hydraulic pump-flow constraints. The Via-Point-based Stochastic Trajectory Optimization (VP-STO) algorithm has demonstrated near-time-optimal hybrid motion planning in this domain, but requires a fixed terminal joint configuration specified prior to optimization. For kinematically redundant manipulators such as forestry cranes, this pre-commitment to a single inverse kinematics solution restricts the planner's ability to exploit redundancy, particularly under the nonlinear, globally coupled pump-flow constraint where admissible joint velocities depend on their combined hydraulic demand. This paper presents TSC-VP-STO, a task-space-constrained extension of VP-STO that replaces the strict terminal joint-space constraint with a task-space constraint, jointly optimizing the trajectory and the redundant degrees of freedom of the terminal configuration. This enables the planner to adapt end configurations to the environment-dependent motion and hydraulic flow allocation, yielding more balanced pump utilization and shorter trajectory durations. We formalize the approach through a configuration space decomposition and derive a concrete reachability constraint for the forestry crane kinematics. Experimental evaluations across multiple planning targets and via-point configurations demonstrates a reduction on trajectory durations by 12-15% on average and improved pump-flow utilization compared to the baseline VP-STO. The practical applicability of TSC-VP-STO is validated through real-world deployment on a forestry crane, including a full log-loading cycle.

</details>


### [414] [Receiver-Centered Robot-to-Human Handover with Grasp-Aware Object Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17839)
*Federico Biagi,Dario Onfiani,Simone Silenzi,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语音驱动的自适应工具交接系统，通过动态调整机械臂末端姿态，优化人机协作中不对称工具的交接体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态工具交接方式在处理非对称工业工具时易导致不自然的抓握，影响协作效率与安全性。

Method: 基于Franka协作机器人，结合大语言模型（LLM）进行意图识别和MediaPipe实现实时3D手部追踪，构建接收者为中心的语音驱动自适应交接框架，动态调整工具姿态。

Result: 实验表明，该自适应系统能显著减少非对称工具的抓取延迟，并提升交互流畅性；同时改善了用户对运动可预测性和任务简单性的信任感知。

Conclusion: 自适应工具交接策略优于静态方法，有助于提升人机协作中的操作效率与用户体验。

Abstract: Collaborative robots are increasingly sharing workspaces with human operators, making tool handover a frequent and safety-critical micro-interaction. However, traditional static handovers often lead to awkward grasps when handling asymmetric industrial tools. This paper presents a receiver-centered voice-driven adaptive handover system for mechanical tools, built on a Franka cobot. Using an LLM for intention recognition and MediaPipe for real-time 3D hand tracking, the framework dynamically adjusts the end-effector's orientation to present tools in an ergonomically optimal, handle-first pose. A within-subjects study compared this adaptive approach with an object-agnostic static baseline. The results demonstrate that the adaptive system reduces the grasp delay for asymmetric tools, improving the fluency of the interaction. Furthermore, the adaptive strategy improved specific trust-related perceptions, particularly motion predictability and perceived task simplicity.

</details>


### [415] [Lifelong Localization in Dynamic Indoor Environments Combining Odometry with Sparse Distance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17852)
*Michael M. Bilevich,Tomer Buber,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于稀疏距离采样和里程计的终身鲁棒定位框架，可在动态室内环境中实现准确定位，成本低且保护隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在动态障碍物的情况下，传统预先构建的地图无法准确用于机器人定位，因此需要一种能够在动态环境中持续鲁棒定位的方法。

Method: 利用机器人的里程计和稀疏的距离采样构建位置先验，并随时间与里程计融合，逐步收敛到真实位姿；同时考虑环境动态变化并加以建模。

Result: 在多个真实室内环境中验证了方法的有效性，仅用16个距离采样即可达到接近SLAM的定位精度，且在静态和正确学习动态特性的环境下均能保证收敛。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了低成本、高隐私、低带宽的鲁棒终身定位，解决了绑架机器人问题，并在理论和实验上证明了其在大范围室内外动态环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Localization is a key task in robot navigation, and many techniques exist for it. In many plausible scenarios, a robot might face unforeseen, dynamic obstacles, rendering any pre-determined map inaccurate for localization. In this work, we propose a robust lifelong localization framework in dynamic planar indoor environments, using the robot's odometry and sparse distance sampling. We demonstrate how distance samples can be used to provide a robust prior on the robot's location. This technique can solve the kidnapped robot problem in real time, up to symmetries. Based on insights from real-world recorded data, we also account for dynamic obstacles. We then fuse this prior, over time, with the odometry to converge to the robot's location. A central property of our method is that it provably converges to the robot's ground truth pose even in large indoor environments when the environment is static. We further show that this guarantee also holds in dynamic environments, as long as the nature of those changes has been correctly learned. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in different real-world indoor environments. In particular, we achieve a localization comparable to SLAM with merely a few (sixteen) distance samples, as opposed to the full LiDAR range. Sufficing with only sparse distance sampling is advantageous in terms of sensor cost, privacy, storage space, and transmission bandwidth.

</details>


### [416] [ConceptTree: Bringing Semantic Transparency to Black-Box Decision Making for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17861)
*Yongyan Wen,Feifan Liu,Jinyi Chen,Bo An,Peng Liu,Siyuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConceptTree是一种基于可解释概念的机器人操作技能选择框架，通过将高层策略表示为视觉观测上的概念级谓词序列，实现透明、可追溯和可干预的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作方法通常将技能选择视为从观测到动作的黑箱映射，缺乏决策透明性，难以支持人类监督与干预。因此需要一种更具可解释性的决策机制。

Method: 提出ConceptTree框架，学习一个基于视觉输入的标准化概念空间，并在此空间上训练决策树来预测高层操作技能，将决策过程显式地表示为概念层次的逻辑规则序列。

Result: 在真实世界复杂长周期操作任务中，ConceptTree优于现有的基于概念的基线方法；定性案例显示可通过修改单个概念实现对决策错误的细粒度干预而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: ConceptTree提供了可解释、可追溯且可干预的高层技能决策机制，增强了人类对长周期机器人操作的监督与控制能力。

Abstract: Establishing interpretable decision-making processes in long-horizon robotic manipulation is critical for enabling reliable human oversight and intervention. However, existing approaches to robotic manipulation largely treat skill selection as opaque mappings from observations to actions, offering limited transparency into how decisions are formed. In this work, we propose ConceptTree, a framework that reframes high-level manipulation skill selection as reasoning over human-interpretable concepts, representing high-level policies as a sequence of concept-level predicates over visual observations. Rather than relying on implicit latent representations, our method learns a normalized concept space grounded in visual inputs, over which a decision tree is trained to predict high-level skills. This formulation yields a transparent decision process that is both traceable and intervenable, enabling direct inspection and modification of policy behavior. We evaluate our approach on a set of real-world robotic manipulation tasks with increasing complexity. Experimental results show that ConceptTree consistently outperforms existing concept-based baselines, particularly in complex, long-horizon scenarios. Furthermore, we provide qualitative case studies showing that our model supports fine-grained intervention by modifying individual concepts, enabling targeted correction of decision errors without retraining.

</details>


### [417] [Leveraging Two Robotic Arms for Tight Assembly Performance Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17876)
*Dror Livnat,Yuval Lavi,Michael M. Bilevich,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CAD模型的双机械臂协同装配操作的端到端框架，显著提升了装配效率和轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对紧凑装配任务中单臂系统效率低、路径规划困难的问题，探索双臂协同的优势。

Method: 利用零件的CAD模型及其在装配状态下的相对位置，设计了一种双机械臂协同运动规划与控制的端到端框架，并在仿真和真实机器人上实现。

Result: 实验表明，相比单臂系统，双臂协同可将平均执行时间减少50%以上，生成更高质量的轨迹，并加速有效机器人位姿的搜索；同时给出了机器人工作单元尺寸的理论边界。

Conclusion: 双机械臂协同在紧配合装配任务中具有显著优势，所提框架兼具理论保证与实际有效性，且开源代码和演示视频已公开。

Abstract: We provide a novel end-to-end framework for the execution of an assembly operation by two robotic arms, given the digital CAD models of the parts and their desired relative placement in their assembled state. We analyze and demonstrate the advantages of using two robotic arms simultaneously in tight assembly operations, compared to single-arm systems. Our method is implemented in both simulation and using physical robots. It provides theoretical guarantees on execution time and trajectory accuracy, supported by empirical evidence. In particular, we show that coordinated movement of two arms reduces average execution time by more than 50% compared to using a single arm only, produces higher-quality trajectories, and accelerates the search for valid robot placements. Furthermore, we establish bounds on the required dimensions of the robotic cell. Our open source software together with real-life video demonstrations are available in our project page.

</details>


### [418] [Manifold-Guided Motion Planning for Tight Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17898)
*Dror Livnat,Michael M. Bilevich,Michal Kleinbort,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CMG-RRT的采样式运动规划算法，专门用于解决刚体装配中的紧间隙问题，通过引导探索临界流形，在多个挑战性任务中实现了100%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于刚体装配中存在严格的几何约束，传统规划方法难以有效处理近零间隙的接触情况，因此需要一种能高效探索接触流形的新型规划器。

Method: 提出临界流形引导的RRT（CMG-RRT），利用构型空间的层次划分，自适应地将采样偏向于临界流形附近，即部件间至少有一个接触点的位姿集合。

Result: 在多个旋转装配基准测试中达到100%成功率，包括首次全自动求解Elk解缠难题，并证明了算法在标准间隙假设下的概率完备性。

Conclusion: CMG-RRT能有效解决紧间隙刚体装配的运动规划问题，其基于临界流形的引导策略显著提升了采样效率和成功率。

Abstract: Motion planning for rigid-body assembly poses a fundamental challenge in robotics due to tight geometric constraints. In such scenarios, feasible motions often require passing through (near-)zero clearance configurations in which the parts are tightly constrained by contact. In this work, we introduce Critical-Manifold Guided RRT (CMG-RRT), a sampling-based planner designed specifically for tight assembly problems. Our key observation is that in tight assemblies, valid solution paths lie on or near a critical manifold: the subset of configuration space consisting of poses with at least one contact point between parts. CMG-RRT guides exploration by adaptively biasing sampling toward neighborhoods of the critical manifold using a hierarchical subdivision of the configuration space. We prove that CMG-RRT is probabilistically complete under standard clearance assumptions. Empirical evaluation on challenging rotational assembly benchmarks demonstrates a 100% success rate across all tested instances, including, to the best of our knowledge, the first fully automatic solution of the Elk disentanglement puzzle. Our open source software is available through our project page: https://www.cgl.cs.tau.ac.il/projects/tight-assembly-planning.

</details>


### [419] [UMCP: A Unified Multi-Task Collaborative Perception Network for Luggage Trolley Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17950)
*Zhirui Sun,Zhihao Jiang,Yao Wang,Jianwei Peng,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于YOLOv12的统一多任务协同感知网络（UMCP），用于机器人自主行李手推车收集，实现检测、关键点定位和方向估计的一体化，显著降低了模型复杂度和计算成本，同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行李手推车视觉感知方法依赖多模型级联推理，导致推理延迟高、部署成本高，难以满足动态复杂环境中实时高精度的需求。

Method: 基于YOLOv12架构，设计统一多任务网络UMCP，融合关键点特征与方向特征，并引入方向特征增强模块（OFEM）和基于KL散度损失的圆形概率分布建模，提升方向估计精度。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在降低模型复杂度和计算成本的同时，取得了具有竞争力的整体精度。

Conclusion: UMCP通过多任务协同和特征增强，有效平衡了精度与效率，适用于机器人在复杂环境中的实时行李手推车感知任务。

Abstract: In robotic autonomous luggage trolley collection, robots must continuously localize scattered luggage trolleys in cluttered and dynamic environments. This requires the vision system to achieve both high accuracy and real-time performance. However, existing visual perception approaches for luggage trolleys often rely on cascaded multi-model inference, leading to increased inference latency and high deployment costs. To address these limitations, this article presents a unified multi-task collaborative perception network (UMCP) that simultaneously performs luggage trolley detection, keypoint detection and orientation estimation. Based on the YOLOv12 architecture, keypoint features are fused with orientation features and then fed into an orientation feature enhancement module (OFEM), thereby improving orientation estimation accuracy. In addition, circular probability distribution modeling with a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence loss is adopted to enhance orientation estimation accuracy further. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive overall accuracy while substantially reducing model complexity and computational cost compared with existing methods. A website about this work is available at https://sites.google.com/view/robot-umcp.

</details>


### [420] [Does Robust VIO Need More Learning? Geometry-Verified Visual Measurements under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17956)
*Yangyang Ning,Shu Liang,Quanbo Ge,Tianchen Deng,Yuhua Qi,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种最小化学习的双目视觉惯性里程计（VIO）框架，仅使用SEA-RAFT生成密集立体匹配和不确定性预测，其余部分如时序跟踪、几何验证和状态估计仍采用显式方法，实验证明该方法在分布偏移下具有良好的鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在部署条件与训练分布不同时，是否需要更深层次的学习来实现鲁棒的VIO，还是可以将学习局限于视觉测量生成。

Method: 采用SEA-RAFT仅生成密集立体匹配和不确定性预测，通过预测的不确定性和立体对极几何一致性对稀疏特征处的密集光流进行采样和过滤，并将其引入滑动窗口的双目惯性估计器中，同时将不确定性传播到立体三角化中用于各向异性的3D高斯映射。

Result: 在EuRoC、VIODE和4Seasons数据集上的实验表明，该方法在运动模糊、动态场景、光照变化以及大范围室内外分布偏移下均表现出准确且稳定的估计性能。消融实验显示，单纯依赖学习到的光流不足以提升性能，关键在于将学习到的对应关系提议与几何验证和不确定性感知加权相结合。

Conclusion: 对于分布外鲁棒的VIO，精心集成的学习视觉测量比学习更大比例的估计流程更为有效。

Abstract: Learning is increasingly introduced into visual-inertial odometry (VIO), ranging from learned feature front-ends to learning-dominant motion and geometry estimation. However, learning more of the pipeline does not necessarily improve robustness when deployment conditions differ from the training distribution. This work asks whether robust VIO under distribution shift truly requires deeper learned estimation, or whether learning can be confined to visual measurement generation. We propose a minimal-learning stereo VIO framework in which SEA-RAFT is used only to propose dense stereo correspondences and predict their uncertainty, while temporal tracking, geometric verification, and state estimation remain explicit. Dense flow is sampled at sparse feature locations, filtered using predicted uncertainty and stereo epipolar consistency, and incorporated into a sliding-window stereo-inertial estimator through uncertainty-weighted reprojection factors. The same uncertainty is further propagated through stereo triangulation for downstream anisotropic 3D Gaussian mapping. Experiments on EuRoC, VIODE, and 4Seasons demonstrate accurate and stable estimation under motion blur, dynamic scenes, illumination changes, and large indoor-to-outdoor distribution shifts. Ablations show that learned flow alone is insufficient: the gains arise from combining learned correspondence proposals with geometric verification and uncertainty-aware weighting. These results suggest that, for OOD-robust VIO, carefully integrated learned visual measurements can be more effective than learning a larger fraction of the estimation pipeline. Code and configs for the benchmark will be open-source upon acceptance. A supplementary video is available at https://drive.google.com/file/d/1EVRhOkhanmNXHbQS1Vr80FoEIAYOYOV2/view

</details>


### [421] [MEVION: Low-Cost Open-Source Data Collection System for Powerful and High-Speed Dual-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17970)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Yoshiki Obinata,Hirokazu Ishida,Jihoon Oh,Temma Suzuki,Shintaro Inoue,Keita Yoneda,Ayumu Iwata,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MEVION是一种低成本、开源的双臂机器人数据采集系统，相比ALOHA具有更高的力和速度输出能力，适用于重物操作和快速操控任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂机器人数据采集系统如ALOHA在处理重物或高速操作时受限于力和速度输出能力，难以满足复杂操作需求。

Method: 设计并构建了名为MEVION的双臂系统，采用电商可购零件和钣金焊接结构以降低成本，使用类似四足机器人的闭链机构减少远端质量，并配备四个6自由度机械臂和并联夹爪。

Result: MEVION单臂重7.0公斤，最大扭矩达60 Nm，整套系统成本约14,000美元，能够实现以往无法完成的操作任务数据采集，并支持基于模仿学习的运动生成。

Conclusion: MEVION在保持低成本和开源特性的同时，显著提升了双臂机器人系统的力与速度性能，拓展了可研究的操作任务范围。

Abstract: The global competition for developing robotic foundation models is intensifying. Among the data collection systems used for dual-arm robots, ALOHA is representative of being low-cost and open-source, and is widely adopted by researchers as a de facto standard. However, due to its limited ability to generate high forces and speeds, it is difficult to handle heavy objects or perform fast manipulations. To address this, we developed MEVION, a low-cost and open-source dual-arm robot data collection system capable of generating greater force and speed. All parts of this robot can be sourced through e-commerce, and by extensively utilizing sheet metal welding, its large body structure is constructed with a small number of components at low cost, while also simplifying assembly. MEVION is equipped with four 6-DoF arms with parallel grippers. Each arm weighs 7.0 kg and has a maximum torque of 60 Nm, and the entire system can be constructed for about USD 14,000. The elbow joint adopts a closed-link mechanism similar to those used in quadruped robots, which reduces the distal mass and enables higher force and speed output at the end-effector. We demonstrate that MEVION enables data collection for object manipulation tasks not previously possible and supports imitation learning-based motion generation. All hardware and software of this work are included in the Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevion.

</details>


### [422] [RynnBrain 1.1: Towards More Capable and Generalizable Embodied Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17977)
*Kehan Li,Bohan Hou,Minghao Zhu,Tianyi Zhang,Zesen Cheng,Zhikai Wang,Sicong Leng,Xin Li,Xiao Lin,Biying Yao,Minghua Zeng,Jiangpin Liu,Ronghao Dang,Jiayan Guo,Siteng Huang,Haoyu Zhao,Heng Ping,Yaxi Zhao,Kexiang Wang,Tong Lu,Shengke Xue,Jiahao Tang,Yulei Wang,Zejing Wang,Jianwei Gao,Shijian Lu,Chengju Liu,Jianfei Yang,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnBrain 1.1 是一系列具身基础模型，支持感知、空间推理、定位与规划，在多个基准上表现优异，并成功应用于真实机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升具身智能体对物理环境的理解与操作能力，实现更高效的跨形态动作控制和多任务学习。

Method: 采用统一的时空和物理感知训练框架，引入接触点预测和原生3D定位，并设计跨具身动作空间与具身掩码机制。

Result: RynnBrain 1.1 在 VSI-Bench、MMSI 和 RefSpatial-Bench 上优于现有模型；真实机器人实验显示其初始化策略优于基于 Qwen 和通用视觉语言动作模型的方法。

Conclusion: RynnBrain 1.1 通过统一建模和多具身训练显著提升了具身认知与操控性能，具备良好的实际部署能力。

Abstract: We present RynnBrain 1.1, a family of embodied foundation models spanning 2B, 9B, and 122B-A10B scales. Trained with a unified spatio-temporal and physically grounded framework, RynnBrain 1.1 supports embodied perception, spatial reasoning, localization, and planning. Compared with RynnBrain 1.0, it further introduces contact-point prediction across the model family and native 3D grounding for the 2B and 9B models, yielding representations and outputs that are more directly aligned with robot manipulation. We also develop RynnBrain-VLA with a unified cross-embodiment action space and embodiment-specific masking, and deploy it on Unitree G1, Astribot-S1, and Tianji-Wuji. RynnBrain 1.1 achieves strong results on embodied cognition, localization, and 3D grounding, with the 122B-A10B model outperforming all evaluated proprietary and open-source models on VSI-Bench, MMSI, and RefSpatial-Bench. Real-robot experiments show that RynnBrain-initialized policies outperform Qwen-based and representative generalist VLAs, while joint multi-task and multi-embodiment training improves process scores and success rates over per-task training.

</details>


### [423] [Distilling Global Traversability Priors for Image-based Affordance Prediction in Off-road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17984)
*Matthew Sivaprakasam,Samuel Triest,Micah Nye,Deegan Atha,Shehryar Khattak,David Fan,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从第一人称视角图像中提取长距离可通行边界的导航方法，利用卫星图像监督网络训练，提升了非结构化地形中远距离越野导航的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的自主导航方法在长视野场景中容易出现短视行为，受限于传感器感知范围，导致机器人决策次优。

Method: 通过利用卫星图像为图像/位姿对数据集计算可行路径，并以此监督网络训练，直接从第一人称视角图像中提取长距离可通行边界。

Result: 在多个离线基准测试中性能提升超过10%，并在真实世界实验中减少了人为干预次数。

Conclusion: 该方法有效恢复了超出局部地图范围的导航信息，显著提高了长距离非结构化地形导航的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Standard methods for autonomous navigation in unstructured terrain are prone to myopic behaviors in long-horizon scenarios. The use of metric maps built from LiDAR or cameras provides necessary local geometry and semantic information but is strictly limited by depth sensing range. By discarding data beyond the mapping horizon robots suffer from suboptimal, short-sighted decisions. To recover this lost information, we focus on extracting long-range traversability-aware frontiers directly from first-person-view (FPV) images. By leveraging satellite imagery, we compute the set of feasible navigation paths for a dataset of image/pose pairs and use them to supervise our network, reducing the need for extensive human demonstration data. We demonstrate that this approach improves performance in long-range off-road navigation over existing methods by more than 10% in various offline benchmarks and reduces the number of human interventions incurred in a set of real-world experiments. More details can be found at https://theairlab.org/ss_frontiers_iros .

</details>


### [424] [Closing the Loop in Humanoid VLA: Persistent 3D Object Tokens for Verifiable Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18016)
*Peng Ren,Haoyang Ge,Jiang Zhao,Cong Huang,Yukun Shi,Pei Chi,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“持久对象标记化”（POT）的方法，用于改善人形机器人在长视野操作任务中的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）控制性能。通过维护基于RGB-D观测的3D对象记录并将其转化为动作专家可用的对象标记，POT-VLA实现了闭环执行系统，使对象状态既可操作又可验证，显著提升了任务成功率，尤其是在需要维持三维空间关系的任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作策略在处理长视野、涉及移动、接触、遮挡和恢复的人形机器人操作任务时，难以将任务对象视为持续存在的物理实体，导致对象状态在动作生成与执行验证之间出现不一致（即对象状态发散问题）。为解决这一问题，本文研究了如何建立对对象状态的持久性表征。

Method: 提出“持久对象标记化”（POT）方法，通过RGB-D感知构建角色索引的3D对象记录，并将这些记录统一转化为对象标记输入至全身动作专家网络。在POT-VLA实现中，相同的对象记录同时用于动作生成和几何谓词检查，形成闭环控制系统，确保对象状态在整个执行过程中保持一致且可验证。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上的实验表明，POT-VLA将基线GR00T-N1.7的80个任务成功数从39提升至71；在外部分析Being-0对齐的服务任务中，POT-VLA取得44/50的成功率，优于Being-0原论文报告的37/50。性能提升最显著的是那些需要长期维持3D空间关系的任务。

Conclusion: 持久的对象中心化状态表示是实现可验证人形机器人VLA控制的有效抽象方式，POT框架有效缓解了对象状态发散问题，为复杂loco-manipulation任务提供了更鲁棒的状态一致性保障。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies are a promising foundation for general robot control, but long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires the robot to treat task objects as persistent physical entities across movement, contact, occlusion, and recovery. We study this problem as object-state divergence: the object state used to condition a whole-body action can differ from the state used to decide whether the action achieved the intended physical relation. We propose \emph{Persistent Object Tokenization} (POT), which maintains role-indexed 3D object records from RGB-D observations and converts them into object tokens for a whole-body action expert. Instantiated as \emph{POT-VLA}, the same object records condition action generation and support geometric predicate checks, yielding a closed-loop execution system in which object state is both actionable and verifiable. On a Unitree G1, POT-VLA improves a matched direct GR00T-N1.7 baseline from 39/80 to 71/80 successes over eight real-world task families. In an external Being-0-aligned reference, POT-VLA achieves 44/50 successes on aligned service tasks, compared with the 37/50 success reported by the Being-0 paper. The largest gains occur on tasks requiring maintained 3D relations, suggesting that persistent object-centered state is a useful abstraction for verifiable humanoid VLA execution.

</details>


### [425] [RoboHarness: Memory-Driven Orchestration of Heterogeneous Robot Policies for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18060)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Zhanguang Zhang,Mark Coates,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboHarness 是一个统一框架，用于整合多种异构机器人控制策略作为可重用的智能体技能，通过多模态执行记忆和在线证据实现能力感知的任务分解与路由，并利用 Memory Bridge 稳定策略交接，显著提升长视野规划的零样本性能和分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划方法通常基于同质化、预定义的技能，难以应对长视野机器人任务中多样化的技能需求和跨策略分布差异，且缺乏对策略能力边界不确定性的建模。

Method: 提出 RoboHarness 框架，将独立开发的控制策略封装为智能体技能；使用多模态执行记忆和在线证据刻画策略的能力边界，进行能力感知的任务分解与路由；通过 Memory Bridge 检索下一策略的历史轨迹，估计其分布内状态区域并引导当前策略向该区域过渡，无需联合重训练。

Result: 在三个公开基准、500个自定义任务和135次真实机器人实验中验证了方法的有效性，实现了高效的能力感知路由和稳定的策略协同，显著提升了零样本长视野任务成功率和分布外鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RoboHarness 提供了一种通用、灵活且稳定的方式来协调异构机器人策略，克服了策略边界不确定和分布不匹配的问题，为复杂长视野任务提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks require diverse capabilities that no single policy can reliably provide. Heterogeneous policies offer complementary strengths, but orchestrating them requires reasoning over uncertain capability boundaries and cross-policy distribution mismatch, which are largely overlooked by existing planning methods built on homogeneous, predefined skills with fixed applicability. We propose RoboHarness, a unified framework that encapsulates independently developed robot control systems as reusable agentic skills. Although instantiated in this work with VLAs, RL policies, and task-and-motion planning (TAMP) systems, RoboHarness is designed as a general framework compatible with a broader range of robot policies, such as navigation policies, model predictive controllers, and world-action models. RoboHarness uses multi-modal execution memory and online evidence to characterize policy capability boundaries for capability-aware decomposition and routing. To stabilize policy handoffs, its Memory Bridge retrieves execution trajectories associated with the next policy, estimates its in-distribution state region, and guides the robot toward that region without joint policy retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, 500 customized tasks, and 135 real-robot experiments demonstrate effective capability-aware routing and stable policy orchestration, yielding substantial improvements in zero-shot long-horizon planning and out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [426] [UniETP: Unifying Environments for Generalizable Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18062)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Ziyou Wang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniETP，一个用于具身任务规划的统一接口，整合了四个常用模拟器，实现了观察和动作空间的标准化，并构建了一个多样化、复杂度递增的新数据集，以推动可泛化模型的发展与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务规划的研究因各模拟器之间格式、动作类型和任务领域的不统一而导致模型难以比较和扩展，缺乏统一标准阻碍了领域发展。

Method: 提出UniETP框架，将AI2-THOR、VirtualHome、Habitat和BEHAVIOR四个模拟器统一到一致的观察与动作空间下，设计通用评估系统，并通过自动化方法构建多难度层级的任务数据集。

Result: 在新基准上对多种最新模型进行了广泛实验，验证了UniETP在促进跨环境任务规划评估方面的有效性，并识别出现有模型在任务逻辑理解、实例定位和指令解析等方面的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: UniETP为具身任务规划提供了标准化且多样化的平台，有助于推动通用性智能体的发展，未来工作可基于此框架进一步提升模型的泛化能力和复杂任务处理水平。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the problem of Embodied Task Planning, where an agent is required to execute a sequence of atomic actions within an interactive environment to complete a user-specified task. Though a variety of simulators and datasets have previously been built for this task, these efforts are largely isolated, with each using its own observation format, action type, and task domain. This fragmentation complicates comprehensive model evaluation and hinders the scalability of training data. As an effort towards generalizable embodied planning, we propose UniETP, a unified interface integrating four commonly-used simulators (AI2-THOR, VirtualHome, Habitat, BEHAVIOR). UniETP is characterized by both standardization and diversity. On one hand, it formalizes all the simulators into a consistent observation and action space, and builds an evaluation system to support complicated task goal. On the other hand, it enhances task diversity and complexity across dimensions like task logic, instance grounding, and instruction understanding, constructing a new dataset with varied levels of difficulty in an automatic manner. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark are conducted to evaluate the embodied planning capabilities of recent models and analyze the performance bottlenecks. Codes and data will be available at https://github.com/woyut/UniETP .

</details>


### [427] [Technical Design Review of Duke Robotics Club's Oogway & Crush: AUVs for RoboSub 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18075)
*Patrick Zheng,Saagar Arya,Hung Le,Mathew Chu,Nathanael Ren,Niko Weaver,Isabella Chen,Jill Wang,Raine Cheng,Siddharth Kini,Avrick Altmann,Srinath Iyer,Ivan Chen,Ian Suh,Parker Jones,Pierson Jones,Sebastian deSouza,Suhaani Sriram,Suvas Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Duke Robotics Club's AUVs Oogway and Crush for RoboSub 2026 expand scope to cover all four design goals—movement, vision, manipulation, and acoustic tracking—through mechanical, electrical, software, and testing improvements to enhance reliability and autonomy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To achieve all four RoboSub design goals (movement, vision, manipulation, acoustic tracking) by improving system reliability and expanding capabilities beyond previous years’ focused scope.

Method: Mechanical upgrades include additional thrusters and CFD-optimized casing; electrical improvements address connection reliability and thruster control; acoustics redesigned with custom PCB and higher-order filters; software enhancements in state estimation, object detection, task planning, and IVC; expanded testing infrastructure.

Result: Improved pitch stability, reliable pinger detection, more capable autonomous operation, and ability to perform a broader set of tasks with high reliability during limited pool time.

Conclusion: The integrated hardware, software, and testing advancements enable Oogway and Crush to reliably tackle the full range of RoboSub challenges, marking a significant step toward robust autonomous underwater performance.

Abstract: The Duke Robotics Club presents Oogway and Crush, our AUVs for RoboSub 2026. This year's strategy expands on our previously narrowed scope, targeting all four of RoboSub's design goals for the first time: movement, vision, manipulation, and acoustic tracking. This expansion is based on sustained reliability investment across all three subsystems. Mechanically, Crush gained two additional thrusters and a CFD-optimized case, providing pitch stability. Electrically, we addressed accumulated failure points by repairing unreliable connections and upgraded thruster control hardware. We also redesigned our acoustics system, adding a new custom PCB with higher-order filters, significantly improving pinger detection reliability. On the software side, improvements to state estimation, sonar-based object detection, vision-driven task planning, and IVC enable more capable and coordinated autonomous runs. Paired with investments in our testing infrastructure to maximize our limited pool time, we can now attempt a broader set of tasks while maintaining the reliability our competition strategy demands.

</details>


### [428] [Importance Sampling and PCA for Finding Failures in Commercial Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18106)
*Hailey Warner,Duncan Eddy,Shreya Parjan,Caroline Cahilly,Harrison Delecki,Matthias Kleinstauber,Chaitanya Shinde,Jerry Lopez,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文将两种罕见事件发现算法（AST和DiFS）应用于商业自动驾驶卡车系统，发现在并道和切入场景中传统蒙特卡洛模拟无法发现的碰撞，并通过基于PCA的统计分析对失败模式进行分类和诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有罕见故障发现方法多在简单学术驾驶系统中验证，难以确定其是否适用于商业级鲁棒规划器，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 应用自适应压力测试（AST）和扩散式故障采样（DiFS）两种算法于商业自动驾驶卡车系统，并结合主成分分析（PCA）对发现的故障进行聚类与逆变换，提取通用噪声轨迹。

Result: 两种算法均能在并道和车辆切入场景中发现传统蒙特卡洛模拟未能发现的模拟碰撞，且通过PCA聚类可识别关键时间步和常见故障模式，重构的噪声轨迹可在相同或相似场景中复现故障。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从故障发现到感知层面缺陷系统性诊断的有效路径，提升了复杂自动驾驶系统中罕见故障分析的实用性与可操作性。

Abstract: Methods for discovering rare failures in autonomous systems have so far been demonstrated almost exclusively in simulations with simple, academic driving stacks, leaving open whether they generalize to the more robust planners used in commercial systems. We address this gap by applying two rare-event discovery algorithms to a commercial autonomous trucking stack. Adaptive stress testing (AST) uses reinforcement learning to search for the most likely noise trajectories leading to a simulated collision, while diffusion-based failure sampling (DiFS) trains a denoising diffusion model to sample a diverse set of failures. We show that both algorithms find simulated collisions during merge and cut-in maneuvers where traditional Monte Carlo simulation does not. To make these failures actionable, we introduce a statistical analysis based on principal component analysis (PCA) that classifies failures into common modes and identifies the timesteps that most influence the outcome. We cluster the principal components and invert the PCA transform to recover generalized noise trajectories, and show that these trajectories reproduce failures in identical and similar scenarios. This provides a path from failure discovery to systematic diagnosis of perception-level flaws.

</details>


### [429] [Isaac Sim-to-Real: Reinforcement Learning based Locomotion for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18135)
*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的鲁棒运动控制框架，利用Nvidia Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab进行零样本仿真到现实迁移，并在Unitree Go1四足机器人上验证了其优异的运动跟踪和抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习在仿真中表现良好但迁移到真实系统时性能下降的sim-to-real gap问题。

Method: 采用Nvidia Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab作为仿真和强化学习训练平台，设计用于全身控制的强化学习框架，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在Unitree Go1机器人上成功实现了零样本部署，实验结果显示该策略在速度跟踪方面与原有控制器相当，且具备更强的抗大扰动恢复能力，达到2.0 m/s的线速度和1.8 rad/s的角速度。

Conclusion: 所提出的强化学习框架能有效缩小sim-to-real差距，实现高性能的四足机器人全身运动控制，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to locomotion have risen in popularity in recent years, showing the capability for complex legged locomotion and whole-body control. Reinforcement learning (RL), the primary learning-based approach for locomotion, often utilizes a high-performance simulation tool, providing a controlled and efficient training and development environment. However, policies that perform well in simulation frequently encounter unexpected challenges when deployed on a physical system, known as the sim-to-real gap. This work presents a robust RL locomotion framework capable of whole-body control. The proposed RL framework utilizes Nvidia's new set of simulation tools, Isaac Sim, and its companion RL framework, Isaac Lab, for training, achieving a zero-shot sim-to-real policy. The performance of our policy is validated on physical hardware using the Unitree Go1, with experimental results showing similar velocity tracking performance to the quadruped's integrated controller, with a greater ability to recover from large disturbances, and achieve linear velocities of 2.0 m/s and angular velocities of 1.8 rad/s.

</details>


### [430] [World Translation: Minimizing Sim-to-Real Gap with Backward Dynamics Extraction and Unpaired Domain Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18154)
*Xinchen Yao,Leixin Chang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“World Translation”的新方法，通过从观测到的转换中反向提取不可观测的动力学信息，并将其在仿真与现实之间进行跨域翻译，从而提升机器人策略在真实世界中的迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习动力学模型的方法在部分可观测环境下表现不佳，尤其是当观察历史无法提供足够信息时（如突发接触事件），难以恢复不可观测因素，导致仿真到现实的迁移效果受限。

Method: 利用仿真器确定性但物理不完美的特性与学习模型准确但在部分可观测下不确定的互补优势，从观测到的转换反向提取不可观测的动力学信息，并将此建模为无配对域翻译问题，在保持动力学内容的同时转移域风格。

Result: 在人形、四足和机械臂平台上实验表明，该方法比基线更准确地建模动力学，尤其在观察历史无法恢复不可观测因素时提升显著；在Go2四足机器人上的真实部署验证了策略迁移性能的改善。

Conclusion: World Translation通过结合仿真与学习模型的优势，有效缓解了部分可观测性带来的挑战，提升了仿真训练策略在真实机器人上的适用性和准确性。

Abstract: The gap between simulation and reality remains a fundamental challenge in deploying simulation-trained robotic policies in the real world. Real-to-sim methods narrow this gap from the real side, learning transition dynamics from real data to build a more realistic digital world. Learned dynamics models are their dominant instance. Such methods, however, face a partial observability problem: the same observation may branch to different transitions due to unobservable factors. Existing methods assume these factors can be recovered from observation history. However, this may fail whenever observation history is uninformative, such as a sudden contact event with no prior warning. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{World Translation}, which exploits a complementary strength of simulators and learned dynamics. Simulators are deterministic but physically imperfect, while learned models are accurate but underdetermined under partial observability. Rather than predicting transitions forward from history, we extract the unobservable dynamics information backward from an observed transition, then translate this feature across simulation and reality as an unpaired domain-translation problem that preserves dynamics content while transferring domain style. Experiments across humanoid, quadruped, and manipulator platforms show that our method achieves more accurate dynamics modeling than baselines, with the largest gains when unobservable factors cannot be recovered from observation history. Real-robot deployment on Go2 quadruped confirms improved policy transfer.

</details>


### [431] [Imitation of Arm Gestures by the Semi-Humanoid Robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18197)
*Anastasiya Ihnatovich,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何分析和MediaPipe姿态估计模型的半人形机器人NICO模仿人类手臂手势的方法，能够从单目RGB输入生成有意义的模仿动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无缝的人机交互需要机器人具备模仿人类手势的能力，而现有人形机器人在该任务中仍面临挑战。

Method: 利用MediaPipe框架获取输入RGB帧中人体关键点的3D坐标，通过解析几何关系计算关节角度，并将这些角度映射到NICO机器人的运动配置中执行。

Result: 初步实验表明，该方法能在不同身高的六名参与者展示多种典型手臂动作时生成合理的模仿运动，但在复杂姿势和手腕动作上仍有局限。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效实现仅依赖单目视觉的人类手臂动作模仿，为半人形机器人在HRI中的应用提供了可行方案，但需进一步优化以应对复杂姿态和精细手部运动。

Abstract: Seamless human-robot interaction (HRI) requires a number of perceptual and motor abilities from the robot, one of them being the imitation of human gestures. Humanoid robots have an advantage in HRI thanks to their anthropomorphic features. In this work, we develop a system for imitation of human arm gestures by the semi-humanoid robot NICO based on analytical geometry and a pretrained MediaPipe pose-estimation model. For each input RGB frame, 3D coordinates of relevant human body landmarks, including arm joints and hand keypoints, are obtained using the MediaPipe framework. Joint angles are then computed from these coordinates using derived geometric relations. Finally, the computed angles are properly mapped to NICO's motor configuration and executed in a predefined motion sequence. Preliminary experiments on several representative arm gestures with six participants of different height indicate that the proposed method can produce meaningful imitative motions from monocular RGB input only, while also highlighting limitations in more complex poses and wrist-related movements.

</details>


### [432] [Learning Adaptive Safety Margins for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18200)
*Junyi Hu,Shuaihang Yuan,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于上下文条件的安全评判器，用于在密集室内环境中自适应地选择扩散规划器生成的轨迹，通过分解为安全性、效率和距离约束三项进行优化，在仿真中训练并成功迁移到真实人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定安全边距在复杂室内环境中容易导致路径绕行或因感知偏差引发碰撞，现有扩散规划方法缺乏可靠的轨迹选择机制。

Method: 设计了一个上下文条件的安全评判器，包含安全性（带 Clearance-budget 惩罚和控制屏障函数残差）、效率（平滑性与安全门控绕路比惩罚）和距离约束匹配（锚定 ESDF 实际 Clearance）三项；采用两阶段教师-学生框架，利用仿真中的特权ESDF信息训练后蒸馏为仅依赖感知的选择器。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D数据集上的PointGoal导航任务中，本方法在成功率（SR）和路径长度加权成功率（SPL）上优于扩散、优化和强化学习基线，并实现跨数据集迁移；纯仿真训练的模型成功部署到Unitree G1人形机器人，在未调参情况下完成真实场景导航。

Conclusion: 自适应安全边距的选择机制显著提升了机器人在杂乱环境中的导航性能，所提安全评判器能有效平衡安全性与效率，且具备良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Robots in cluttered indoor spaces often fail not because they cannot generate collision-free paths, but because a fixed safety margin is mis-calibrated: conservative margins cause detours and timeouts, while permissive margins lead to near-boundary shortcuts under perception bias. Diffusion-based planners propose diverse trajectory candidates from egocentric RGB-D, yet reliable selection remains the bottleneck. We propose a context-conditioned safety critic that learns an adaptive clearance preference for ranking diffusion proposals, decomposed into three complementary terms: (i) a safety term with a clearance-budget penalty and a control-barrier-function residual for waypoint- and transition-wise safety, (ii) an efficiency term combining a smoothness penalty with a safety-gated detour-ratio penalty that avoids detours without incentivizing risky shortcuts, and (iii) a distance-constraint matching term that anchors the learned budget to realized ESDF clearances to prevent margin collapse. We train the critic with privileged ESDF geometry in simulation and distill it into a perception-only selector via a two-stage teacher-student procedure. On PointGoal navigation in HM3D and MP3D, including cross-dataset transfer, our method achieves the highest success rate (SR) and success weighted by path length (SPL) among strong diffusion, optimization, and RL baselines. Trained purely in simulation, it transfers to a Unitree G1 humanoid and navigates cluttered indoor scenes without task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [433] [Optimization of sim-to-real transfer in the humanoid robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18210)
*Juraj Gavura,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于校准的抓取管道，结合YOLO目标检测、立体视觉定位和视觉反馈模型，实现了无需RGB-D相机或外部追踪系统的机器人桌面物体抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决仿真到现实的差距导致的机器人抓取定位误差问题，提升在没有高成本设备条件下的抓取成功率。

Method: 采用YOLO进行手部和物体检测，利用机器人内置鱼眼相机进行立体视觉定位，引入非线性校准模型和任务特定修正，并设计视觉反馈模型在抓取前对手部与物体进行对齐。

Result: 非线性校准模型在标定区域内表现最佳，视觉反馈模型在整个桌面工作区实现了最高的整体抓取成功率。

Conclusion: 所提出的校准与视觉反馈相结合的方法有效提升了NICO机器人在低成本配置下的桌面物体抓取性能，无需依赖外部追踪或高端传感设备。

Abstract: Robotic grasping requires accurate coordination between visual perception, object localization, inverse kinematics, and hand control. However, when movements planned in simulation are executed on a physical robot, the sim-to-real gap can cause small positioning errors that prevent successful grasping. In our previous work, we introduced a low-cost haptic calibration method that improved 2D reaching accuracy of the humanoid robot NICO. In this paper, we extend this approach from reaching to tabletop object grasping by adding YOLO-based object and hand detection, stereo vision-based localization using the robot's built-in low-resolution fisheye cameras, and task-specific corrections for grasp execution. Together, these components form a novel calibration-based grasping pipeline that does not require RGB-D cameras, motion capture, or external tracking systems. We also implemented a visual feedback model that aligns the robot hand with the detected object before grasping. Our results show that the fully nonlinear calibration model achieved the best performance inside the calibrated area, while the visual feedback model achieved the highest overall grasping success across the full tabletop workspace.

</details>


### [434] [FM-VLA: Force-based Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18231)
*Ruicheng Li,Qixiu Li,Ruichun Ma,Yu Deng,Lin Luo,Zhiying Du,Jianfeng Xiang,Huizhi Liang,Ruicheng Wang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于力反馈记忆的视觉-语言-动作（FM-VLA）模型，通过引入力历史信息增强机器人在非马尔可夫、接触丰富的操作任务中的时序上下文推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉记忆的VLA模型在处理视觉模糊的时间事件时存在局限性，且计算开销大，难以有效捕捉细微接触变化。

Method: 使用变分自编码器（VAE）对力时间序列进行预训练，将力历史编码为紧凑的力记忆token，并与短状态历史一起作为额外条件输入到动作专家模块中。

Result: 在三个依赖记忆的任务（找隐藏块、按按钮、特定次数擦盘子）中，FM-VLA实现了超过80%的成功率，显著优于基线方法，且推理开销极小。

Conclusion: 力基记忆是一种轻量高效的方式，能够有效提升VLA模型在接触丰富、非马尔可夫环境下的性能，拓展了机器人对时序接触事件的理解与决策能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved impressive generalization in robotic manipulation, and recent memory-augmented VLAs have relaxed the Markovian assumption by conditioning on past images or language summaries. Vision-based memory approaches address this by conditioning on sampled past image frames, but they are computationally expensive and fundamentally limited when temporal events are visually ambiguous, e.g., pushing a button multiple times with small movements. We propose FM-VLA, a VLA model with force-based memory, enabling temporal context reasoning for non-Markovian, contact-rich manipulation. We encode force histories into compact force memory tokens with a variational autoencoder (VAE) pretrained with force time series reconstruction. By projecting force latent representations and short state history as additional conditioning tokens to the action expert module, we enable VLAs to leverage accumulated contact event history to guide manipulation. We evaluate FM-VLA on three memory-dependent tasks, including finding a hidden block, pressing a button, and wiping a dish for a specific number of times. Our lightweight force memory achieves over 80% success rate with minimal inference overhead, significantly outperforming baseline approaches. Project page: https://qft-333.github.io/FM-VLA-Page/

</details>


### [435] [Patch Policy: Efficient Embodied Control via Dense Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18236)
*Gaoyue Zhou,Zichen Jeff Cui,Ada Langford,Bowen Tan,Yann LeCun,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Patch Policy是一种轻量级、高效的架构扩展，使基于Transformer的机器人策略能直接使用预训练的密集视觉特征（如ViT的patch tokens），在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略要么丢失细粒度空间信息，要么依赖从零训练的视觉骨干网络，未能充分利用大规模预训练视觉模型的优势；而能利用密集特征的模型通常过于庞大和缓慢。

Method: 提出Patch Policy，通过引入块因果注意力掩码（block-causal attention mask），在保持时间因果性的同时允许模型关注每个观测中的多个patch token，并融合其他状态信息。

Result: 在四个仿真和三个真实环境套件中，相比使用全局池化表示的最先进方法实现了40%的相对提升，且超越微调后的OpenVLA-OFT达18%，仅使用约0.7%的参数。

Conclusion: Patch Policy为机器人领域提供了一种高效利用持续发展的视觉表征学习成果的新途径，兼顾训练效率与推理速度，适用于高频、反应式控制任务。

Abstract: Pretrained dense visual features from Vision Transformers (ViTs) are powerful yet have been underutilized in robot learning. Modern robot policies either compress each observation into a single global token, or rely on visual backbones trained from scratch, sacrificing both fine-grained spatial detail and the benefits of large-scale visual pre-training. While there exist policies that do operate on dense patch features like large vision-language-action models (VLAs), they tend to be heavy and slow, inheriting the full cost of a billion-parameter vision-language model (VLM) backbone. We close this gap with Patch Policy, a minimal architectural extension that enables transformer-based policies to consume dense pre-trained patch tokens directly without the computational overhead of a full VLM. At its core is a block-causal attention mask that preserves the temporal causality of standard policies while letting the model attend over many patch tokens per observation, alongside other state information. Patch Policy is lightweight, fast, and highly effective. Across four simulated and three real-world environment suites, our method achieves a 40% relative improvement over policies using state-of-the-art global-pooled representations. Furthermore, it surpasses fine-tuned OpenVLA-OFT by 18% while using roughly 0.7% of the parameters. We believe Patch Policy provides a pipeline for the robotics community to readily leverage continuing progress in visual representation learning, without sacrificing the training efficiency or inference speed required for high-frequency, reactive control. Videos can be viewed at https://patch-policy.github.io

</details>
